<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>Provider Lookup Evaluation Report</title>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js@4.4.0/dist/chart.umd.min.js"></script>
<style>
*{margin:0;padding:0;box-sizing:border-box}
body{font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,-apple-system,sans-serif;background:#f0f2f5;color:#2c3e50;line-height:1.6}
.header{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#1a1a2e 0%,#16213e 50%,#0f3460 100%);color:#fff;padding:40px 0;text-align:center}
.header h1{font-size:2.2em;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px}
.header .sub{font-size:1.1em;opacity:.85}.header .meta{margin-top:16px;font-size:.9em;opacity:.7}
.container{max-width:1440px;margin:0 auto;padding:30px 20px}

.kpi-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(190px,1fr));gap:14px;margin-bottom:28px}
.kpi-card{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:22px;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.06);text-align:center;cursor:pointer;transition:all .2s;border:2px solid transparent}
.kpi-card:hover{transform:translateY(-3px);box-shadow:0 6px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.12);border-color:#3498db}
.kpi-val{font-size:2.3em;font-weight:800;margin:6px 0}.kpi-lbl{font-size:.82em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:600}.kpi-sub{font-size:.78em;color:#95a5a6;margin-top:4px}
.kpi-hint{font-size:.7em;color:#3498db;margin-top:6px;opacity:0;transition:opacity .2s}.kpi-card:hover .kpi-hint{opacity:1}

.section{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:28px;margin-bottom:24px;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.06)}
.section h2{font-size:1.4em;margin-bottom:20px;color:#2c3e50;border-bottom:2px solid #3498db;padding-bottom:10px}
.section h3{font-size:1.1em;margin:16px 0 10px;color:#34495e}

.chart-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(420px,1fr));gap:24px;margin-bottom:24px}
.chart-box{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:24px;box-shadow:0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.06)}
.chart-box h3{margin-bottom:16px;font-size:1.1em;color:#2c3e50;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}
.chart-box .click-hint{font-size:.75em;color:#3498db;font-weight:400}

/* Modal */
.modal-overlay{display:none;position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,.55);z-index:1000;justify-content:center;align-items:flex-start;padding:40px 20px;overflow-y:auto}
.modal-overlay.open{display:flex}
.modal{background:#fff;border-radius:16px;width:100%;max-width:1200px;max-height:85vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.3);animation:modalIn .25s ease}
@keyframes modalIn{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-30px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}
.modal-header{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:20px 28px;border-bottom:1px solid #ecf0f1;flex-shrink:0}
.modal-header h3{font-size:1.3em;color:#2c3e50}.modal-header .badge{font-size:.85em;background:#3498db;color:#fff;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:20px;font-weight:600}
.modal-close{background:none;border:none;font-size:1.8em;cursor:pointer;color:#95a5a6;padding:0 8px;transition:color .2s}.modal-close:hover{color:#e74c3c}
.modal-body{overflow-y:auto;padding:20px 28px;flex:1}
.modal-stats{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(120px,1fr));gap:12px;margin-bottom:20px}
.modal-stat{background:#f8f9fa;border-radius:8px;padding:12px;text-align:center}.modal-stat .v{font-size:1.6em;font-weight:700}.modal-stat .l{font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase}

table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.86em}
th{background:#2c3e50;color:#fff;padding:11px 10px;text-align:left;font-weight:600;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:10;cursor:pointer}
th:hover{background:#34495e}
td{padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid #ecf0f1}
tr:hover{background:#ebf5fb !important}
tr:nth-child(even){background:#fafbfc}
.tbl-scroll{max-height:500px;overflow-y:auto;border:1px solid #ecf0f1;border-radius:8px}

.clickable-row{cursor:pointer;transition:background .15s}
.clickable-row:hover{background:#d6eaf8 !important}

.badge-res{background:#27ae60;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600}
.badge-pen{background:#f39c12;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600}
.badge-esc{background:#e74c3c;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600}
.badge-err{background:#c0392b;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600}
.badge-pl{background:#8e44ad;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600}
.badge-unk{background:#95a5a6;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:600}

.finding{background:#f8f9fa;border-left:4px solid #3498db;padding:14px 18px;margin:10px 0;border-radius:0 8px 8px 0;cursor:pointer;transition:all .2s}
.finding:hover{background:#ebf5fb;transform:translateX(4px)}
.finding.success{border-left-color:#27ae60}.finding.warning{border-left-color:#f39c12}.finding.danger{border-left-color:#e74c3c}

.search-bar{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.search-bar input{flex:1;min-width:200px;padding:10px 16px;border:2px solid #ecf0f1;border-radius:8px;font-size:.95em;transition:border-color .2s}
.search-bar input:focus{outline:none;border-color:#3498db}
.search-bar select{padding:10px 16px;border:2px solid #ecf0f1;border-radius:8px;font-size:.95em;background:#fff;cursor:pointer}
.search-bar select:focus{outline:none;border-color:#3498db}
.result-count{font-size:.85em;color:#7f8c8d;margin-bottom:8px}

/* Distortion comparison */
.distortion-panel{margin:16px 0;border:1px solid #ecf0f1;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden}
.distortion-panel-header{background:#34495e;color:#fff;padding:10px 16px;font-weight:600;font-size:.95em}
.distortion-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}
.distortion-table th{background:#ecf0f1;color:#2c3e50;padding:8px 12px;font-size:.82em;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.5px}
.distortion-table td{padding:8px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0f0f0;font-size:.9em}
.distortion-table .changed{background:#fff3cd}
.val-original{color:#7f8c8d}.val-distorted{color:#e74c3c;font-weight:700}.val-same{color:#27ae60}
.val-arrow{color:#95a5a6;margin:0 6px}
.change-tag{display:inline-block;background:#e74c3c;color:#fff;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:12px;font-size:.75em;font-weight:600;margin:2px}
.change-tag.none{background:#27ae60}

.raw-response{background:#2c3e50;color:#ecf0f1;padding:12px 16px;border-radius:8px;font-family:'Cascadia Code',monospace,Consolas;font-size:.82em;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;max-height:200px;overflow-y:auto;margin-top:10px}

@media print{body{background:#fff}.header{background:#2c3e50!important;-webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;print-color-adjust:exact}.section,.chart-box,.kpi-card{box-shadow:none;border:1px solid #ddd}.modal-overlay{display:none!important}}
@media(max-width:768px){.chart-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="header">
    <h1>Provider Lookup Evaluation Report</h1>
    <div class="sub">Distorted Data Evaluation -- Fuzzy Matching & Resolution Analysis</div>
    <div class="meta">Generated: July 17, 2026 at 05:23 PM | Cases: NIGO-UAT-1001 to NIGO-UAT-1300 | Click any chart, card, or row for details</div>
</div>

<div class="container">

<div class="kpi-grid">
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('all')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Total Cases</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#2c3e50">300</div><div class="kpi-sub">70 providers x 13 distortions</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view all</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('valid')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Valid Responses</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#3498db">273</div><div class="kpi-sub">91.0% response rate</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('RESOLVED')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Auto-Resolved</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#27ae60">191</div><div class="kpi-sub">63.7% of total</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('PENDING REVIEW')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Pending Review</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#f39c12">73</div><div class="kpi-sub">24.3% of total</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('ESCALATED')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Escalated</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#e74c3c">9</div><div class="kpi-sub">3.0% of total</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('valid')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Avg Confidence</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#27ae60">85.6%</div><div class="kpi-sub">Across valid responses</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click for details</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('ERROR')"><div class="kpi-lbl">System Errors</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#c0392b">3</div><div class="kpi-sub">Timeouts / failures</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view</div></div>
    <div class="kpi-card" onclick="showKpiDrill('PROMPT_LEAK')"><div class="kpi-lbl">Prompt Leaks</div><div class="kpi-val" style="color:#8e44ad">24</div><div class="kpi-sub">Invalid prompts returned</div><div class="kpi-hint">Click to view</div></div>
</div>

<div class="section">
    <h2>Key Findings <span style="font-size:.7em;color:#3498db;font-weight:400">(click to drill down)</span></h2>
    <div class="finding success" onclick="showCategoryDrill('Control (No Distortion)')"><strong>Control Baseline:</strong> No-distortion cases: <strong>73.9%</strong> resolution, <strong>89.2%</strong> avg confidence.</div>
    <div class="finding success" onclick="showCategoryDrill('Phone Number Change')"><strong>Best:</strong> "Phone Number Change" -- <strong>82.6%</strong> resolution, <strong>90.1%</strong> confidence.</div>
    <div class="finding danger" onclick="showCategoryDrill('Provider Type Change')"><strong>Worst:</strong> "Provider Type Change" -- <strong>17.4%</strong> resolution, <strong>37.7%</strong> confidence.</div>
    <div class="finding warning" onclick="showKpiDrill('failures')"><strong>Reliability:</strong> 3 errors + 24 prompt leaks = <strong>9.0%</strong> failure rate.</div>
</div>

<div class="chart-grid">
    <div class="chart-box"><h3>Status Distribution <span class="click-hint">Click a segment</span></h3><canvas id="statusChart"></canvas></div>
    <div class="chart-box"><h3>Action Distribution <span class="click-hint">Click a segment</span></h3><canvas id="actionChart"></canvas></div>
    <div class="chart-box"><h3>Confidence Distribution <span class="click-hint">Click a bar</span></h3><canvas id="confChart"></canvas></div>
    <div class="chart-box"><h3>Resolution Rate by Distortion <span class="click-hint">Click a bar</span></h3><canvas id="catResChart"></canvas></div>
</div>
<div class="chart-grid">
    <div class="chart-box" style="grid-column:1/-1"><h3>Avg Confidence by Distortion Type <span class="click-hint">Click a bar</span></h3><canvas id="catConfChart" height="100"></canvas></div>
</div>
<div class="chart-grid">
    <div class="chart-box" style="grid-column:1/-1"><h3>Failure Rate by Distortion Type <span class="click-hint">Click a bar</span></h3><canvas id="catFailChart" height="100"></canvas></div>
</div>

<div class="section">
    <h2>Performance by Distortion Category <span style="font-size:.7em;color:#3498db;font-weight:400">(click row to expand)</span></h2>
    <div class="tbl-scroll" style="max-height:none"><table id="catTable"><thead><tr>
        <th></th><th>Category</th><th style="text-align:center">Total</th><th style="text-align:center">Resolved</th><th style="text-align:center">Pending</th><th style="text-align:center">Escalated</th><th style="text-align:center">Errors</th><th style="text-align:center">Leaks</th><th style="text-align:center">Resolution %</th><th style="text-align:center">Avg Conf.</th><th style="text-align:center">Failure %</th>
    </tr></thead><tbody id="catTableBody"></tbody></table></div>
</div>

<div class="section">
    <h2>Detailed Case Results <span style="font-size:.7em;color:#3498db;font-weight:400">(300 cases -- click row for full distortion details)</span></h2>
    <div class="search-bar">
        <input type="text" id="searchInput" placeholder="Search case ref, provider, reasoning..." oninput="filterDetailTable()">
        <select id="filterStatus" onchange="filterDetailTable()"><option value="">All Statuses</option><option value="RESOLVED">Resolved</option><option value="PENDING REVIEW">Pending Review</option><option value="ESCALATED">Escalated</option><option value="ERROR">Error</option><option value="PROMPT_LEAK">Prompt Leak</option></select>
        <select id="filterCategory" onchange="filterDetailTable()"><option value="">All Categories</option></select>
        <select id="filterConfidence" onchange="filterDetailTable()"><option value="">All Confidence</option><option value="HIGH">HIGH (90%+)</option><option value="MEDIUM">MEDIUM (50-89%)</option><option value="LOW">LOW (0-49%)</option></select>
    </div>
    <div class="result-count" id="resultCount"></div>
    <div class="tbl-scroll" id="detailScroll"><table id="detailTable"><thead><tr>
        <th onclick="sortDetailTable('case')">Case Ref</th><th onclick="sortDetailTable('cat')">Distortion</th><th>What Was Changed</th><th onclick="sortDetailTable('expProv')">Expected Provider</th><th onclick="sortDetailTable('matchProv')">Matched Provider</th><th onclick="sortDetailTable('status')">Status</th><th onclick="sortDetailTable('conf')" style="text-align:center">Conf.</th><th onclick="sortDetailTable('action')">Action</th>
    </tr></thead><tbody id="detailTableBody"></tbody></table></div>
</div>

<div class="section">
    <h2>Evaluation Methodology</h2>
    <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:20px">
        <div><h3>Data Preparation</h3><ul style="margin:8px 0 0 20px"><li><strong>70</strong> unique providers from Provider_UAT.txt</li><li><strong>13</strong> distortion scenarios per provider</li><li><strong>910</strong> total cases generated (300 processed)</li></ul></div>
        <div><h3>Targets</h3><table style="margin-top:8px"><thead><tr><th>Metric</th><th>Target</th><th>Actual</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>
            <tr><td>Resolution Rate</td><td>&gt;80%</td><td>63.7%</td><td>FAIL</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Avg Confidence</td><td>&gt;85%</td><td>85.6%</td><td>PASS</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Failure Rate</td><td>&lt;5%</td><td>9.0%</td><td>FAIL</td></tr>
            <tr><td>Control Baseline</td><td>&gt;95%</td><td>73.9%</td><td>FAIL</td></tr>
        </tbody></table></div>
    </div>
</div>
</div>

<!-- DRILL MODAL -->
<div class="modal-overlay" id="modal" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeModal()">
    <div class="modal">
        <div class="modal-header"><h3 id="modalTitle">Details</h3><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px"><span class="badge" id="modalBadge"></span><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeModal()">&times;</button></div></div>
        <div class="modal-body">
            <div class="modal-stats" id="modalStats"></div>
            <div class="tbl-scroll" id="modalTableWrap"><table><thead><tr>
                <th>Case</th><th>Category</th><th>What Changed</th><th>Expected</th><th>Matched</th><th>Status</th><th style="text-align:center">Conf.</th><th>Action</th>
            </tr></thead><tbody id="modalTableBody"></tbody></table></div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<!-- CASE DETAIL MODAL -->
<div class="modal-overlay" id="caseModal" onclick="if(event.target===this)closeCaseModal()">
    <div class="modal" style="max-width:900px">
        <div class="modal-header"><h3 id="caseModalTitle">Case Details</h3><button class="modal-close" onclick="closeCaseModal()">&times;</button></div>
        <div class="modal-body" id="caseModalBody"></div>
    </div>
</div>

<script>
const ALL_CASES = [{"case": "NIGO-UAT-1001", "cat": "First Name Misspelling", "expProv": "Samantha MacDonald", "matchProv": "Samantha Faye MacDonald", "status": "RESOLVED", "conf": 95, "confLvl": "HIGH", "action": "AUTO_RESUBMIT", "reasoning": "Exact registration number match and strong fuzzy name match; const CONF_BUCKETS = {"0-25%": 14, "26-50%": 5, "51-75%": 35, "76-90%": 28, "91-100%": 191};
const CATEGORIES = ["First Name Misspelling", "Last Name Misspelling", "Registration Number Change", "First/Last Name Swap", "Phone Number Change", "Postal Code Change", "Provider Type Change", "Multiple Fields (Name + RegNo)", "Missing First Name", "Missing Registration Number", "Double Letter Typo", "Partial First Name", "Control (No Distortion)"];
const FIELDS = ["First Name","Last Name","Reg Number","Phone","Postal Code","Provider Type"];
const CAT_COLORS = {"First Name Misspelling":"#e74c3c","Last Name Misspelling":"#c0392b","Registration Number Change":"#3498db","First/Last Name Swap":"#f1c40f","Phone Number Change":"#2ecc71","Postal Code Change":"#27ae60","Provider Type Change":"#9b59b6","Multiple Fields (Name + RegNo)":"#e67e22","Missing First Name":"#d35400","Missing Registration Number":"#2980b9","Double Letter Typo":"#e74c3c","Partial First Name":"#8e44ad","Control (No Distortion)":"#1abc9c"};

function statusBadge(s){const m={"RESOLVED":"badge-res","PENDING REVIEW":"badge-pen","ESCALATED":"badge-esc","ERROR":"badge-err","PROMPT_LEAK":"badge-pl"};return `<span class="${m[s]||'badge-unk'}">${s}</span>`;}
function confColor(c){return c>=90?'#27ae60':c>=75?'#f39c12':c>=50?'#e67e22':'#e74c3c';}
function esc(s){const d=document.createElement('div');d.textContent=s;return d.innerHTML;}

function changeTags(changes){
    if(!changes||!changes.length) return '<span class="change-tag none">No changes</span>';
    return changes.map(c=>`<span class="change-tag">${esc(c)}</span>`).join(' ');
}

function changeTagsShort(changes){
    if(!changes||!changes.length) return '<span class="change-tag none">Exact Match</span>';
    return changes.map(c=>{
        const short = c.length>50 ? c.substring(0,47)+'...' : c;
        return `<span class="change-tag" title="${esc(c)}">${esc(short)}</span>`;
    }).join(' ');
}

// Build distortion comparison table for a case
function distortionCompareHTML(c){
    if(!c.orig || !Object.keys(c.orig).length) return '<p style="color:#95a5a6">No distortion data available</p>';
    let rows='';
    FIELDS.forEach(f=>{
        const origVal = c.orig[f]||'(empty)';
        const distVal = c.distorted[f]||'(empty)';
        const changed = origVal!==distVal;
        rows+=`<tr class="${changed?'changed':''}">
            <td style="font-weight:600">${esc(f)}</td>
            <td class="val-original">${esc(origVal)}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center"><span class="val-arrow">${changed?'&rarr;':'='}</span></td>
            <td class="${changed?'val-distorted':'val-same'}">${esc(distVal)}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center">${changed?'<span class="change-tag">CHANGED</span>':'<span class="change-tag none">SAME</span>'}</td>
        </tr>`;
    });
    return `<div class="distortion-panel">
        <div class="distortion-panel-header">Original vs Distorted Field Values</div>
        <table class="distortion-table"><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th>Original Value</th><th></th><th>Distorted (Submitted)</th><th>Status</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>
    </div>`;
}

// ============================================================
// MODAL FUNCTIONS
// ============================================================
function openModal(title,badge,cases){
    document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent=title;
    document.getElementById('modalBadge').textContent=badge;
    const res=cases.filter(c=>c.status==='RESOLVED').length;
    const pen=cases.filter(c=>c.status==='PENDING REVIEW').length;
    const esca=cases.filter(c=>c.status==='ESCALATED').length;
    const errs=cases.filter(c=>c.status==='ERROR'||c.status==='PROMPT_LEAK').length;
    const validC=cases.filter(c=>c.status!=='ERROR'&&c.status!=='PROMPT_LEAK');
    const avgC=validC.length?Math.round(validC.reduce((a,c)=>a+c.conf,0)/validC.length*10)/10:0;
    document.getElementById('modalStats').innerHTML=`
        <div class="modal-stat"><div class="v" style="color:#2c3e50">${cases.length}</div><div class="l">Total</div></div>
        <div class="modal-stat"><div class="v" style="color:#27ae60">${res}</div><div class="l">Resolved</div></div>
        <div class="modal-stat"><div class="v" style="color:#f39c12">${pen}</div><div class="l">Pending</div></div>
        <div class="modal-stat"><div class="v" style="color:#e74c3c">${esca}</div><div class="l">Escalated</div></div>
        <div class="modal-stat"><div class="v" style="color:#c0392b">${errs}</div><div class="l">Errors/Leaks</div></div>
        <div class="modal-stat"><div class="v" style="color:${confColor(avgC)}">${avgC}%</div><div class="l">Avg Conf</div></div>`;
    let rows='';
    cases.forEach(c=>{
        rows+=`<tr class="clickable-row" onclick="showCaseDetail('${c.case}')">
            <td style="font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap">${esc(c.case)}</td><td>${esc(c.cat)}</td>
            <td>${changeTagsShort(c.changes)}</td>
            <td>${esc(c.expProv)}</td><td>${esc(c.matchProv)}</td>
            <td>${statusBadge(c.status)}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:${confColor(c.conf)}">${c.conf}%</td>
            <td>${esc(c.action)}</td></tr>`;
    });
    document.getElementById('modalTableBody').innerHTML=rows;
    document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('open');
}
function closeModal(){document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('open');}

function showCaseDetail(caseRef){
    const c=ALL_CASES.find(x=>x.case===caseRef);
    if(!c)return;
    document.getElementById('caseModalTitle').textContent=c.case+' -- '+c.cat;
    const sjStr = c.sj ? JSON.stringify(c.sj,null,2) : 'N/A';
    document.getElementById('caseModalBody').innerHTML=`
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px">
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Case Reference</div><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:1.1em;margin-top:2px">${esc(c.case)}</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Distortion Category</div><div style="margin-top:2px"><span style="display:inline-block;width:12px;height:12px;background:${CAT_COLORS[c.cat]||'#95a5a6'};border-radius:2px;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle"></span>${esc(c.cat)}</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Expected Provider</div><div style="margin-top:2px">${esc(c.expProv)}</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Matched Provider</div><div style="font-weight:600;margin-top:2px">${esc(c.matchProv)}</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Status</div><div style="margin-top:4px">${statusBadge(c.status)}</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Confidence</div><div style="font-weight:700;font-size:1.3em;color:${confColor(c.conf)};margin-top:2px">${c.conf}% (${c.confLvl})</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Action</div><div style="font-weight:600;margin-top:2px">${esc(c.action)}</div></div>
            <div><div style="font-size:.75em;color:#7f8c8d;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:600">Reasoning</div><div style="margin-top:2px;font-size:.9em">${esc(c.reasoning||'N/A')}</div></div>
        </div>

        <h3 style="font-size:1em;margin:16px 0 8px;color:#2c3e50;border-bottom:1px solid #ecf0f1;padding-bottom:6px">Distortion Details -- What Was Changed</h3>
        <div style="margin-bottom:12px">${changeTags(c.changes)}</div>
        ${distortionCompareHTML(c)}

        <h3 style="font-size:1em;margin:20px 0 8px;color:#2c3e50;border-bottom:1px solid #ecf0f1;padding-bottom:6px">Submitted JSON (as sent to the system)</h3>
        <div class="raw-response">${esc(sjStr)}</div>

        <h3 style="font-size:1em;margin:20px 0 8px;color:#2c3e50;border-bottom:1px solid #ecf0f1;padding-bottom:6px">Raw System Response</h3>
        <div class="raw-response">${esc(c.raw)}</div>`;
    document.getElementById('caseModal').classList.add('open');
}
function closeCaseModal(){document.getElementById('caseModal').classList.remove('open');}

function showKpiDrill(type){
    let cases,title,badge;
    if(type==='all'){cases=ALL_CASES;title='All Cases';badge=cases.length+' cases';}
    else if(type==='valid'){cases=ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.status!=='ERROR'&&c.status!=='PROMPT_LEAK');title='Valid Responses';badge=cases.length+' cases';}
    else if(type==='failures'){cases=ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.status==='ERROR'||c.status==='PROMPT_LEAK');title='Failures (Errors + Prompt Leaks)';badge=cases.length+' cases';}
    else{cases=ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.status===type);title=type+' Cases';badge=cases.length+' cases';}
    openModal(title,badge,cases);
}
function showCategoryDrill(cat){openModal('Category: '+cat,ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.cat===cat).length+' cases',ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.cat===cat));}

// ============================================================
// CATEGORY TABLE
// ============================================================
function buildCatTable(){
    const tbody=document.getElementById('catTableBody');let html='';
    Object.keys(CAT_METRICS).forEach(cat=>{
        const m=CAT_METRICS[cat];const color=CAT_COLORS[cat]||'#95a5a6';
        html+=`<tr class="clickable-row" onclick="showCategoryDrill('${cat}')" title="Click to see cases">
            <td><span style="display:inline-block;width:14px;height:14px;background:${color};border-radius:3px"></span></td>
            <td style="font-weight:600">${esc(cat)}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center">${m.total}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;color:#27ae60;font-weight:700">${m.resolved}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;color:#f39c12;font-weight:700">${m.pending}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;color:#e74c3c;font-weight:700">${m.escalated}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;color:#c0392b;font-weight:700">${m.errors}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;color:#8e44ad;font-weight:700">${m.prompt_leaks}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:${m.resRate>70?'#27ae60':m.resRate>50?'#f39c12':'#e74c3c'}">${m.resRate}%</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:${confColor(m.avgConf)}">${m.avgConf}%</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:${m.failRate>10?'#e74c3c':m.failRate>5?'#f39c12':'#27ae60'}">${m.failRate}%</td>
        </tr>`;
    });
    tbody.innerHTML=html;
}

// ============================================================
// DETAIL TABLE (searchable, sortable, with distortion tags)
// ============================================================
let detailSortField='case';let detailSortAsc=true;
function buildDetailTable(cases){
    const tbody=document.getElementById('detailTableBody');let html='';
    cases.forEach(c=>{
        const catColor=CAT_COLORS[c.cat]||'#95a5a6';
        html+=`<tr class="clickable-row" onclick="showCaseDetail('${c.case}')">
            <td style="font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap">${esc(c.case)}</td>
            <td><span style="display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;background:${catColor};border-radius:2px;margin-right:4px"></span>${esc(c.cat)}</td>
            <td style="max-width:300px">${changeTagsShort(c.changes)}</td>
            <td>${esc(c.expProv)}</td><td>${esc(c.matchProv)}</td>
            <td>${statusBadge(c.status)}</td>
            <td style="text-align:center;font-weight:700;color:${confColor(c.conf)}">${c.conf}%</td>
            <td>${esc(c.action)}</td></tr>`;
    });
    tbody.innerHTML=html;
    document.getElementById('resultCount').textContent=`Showing ${cases.length} of ${ALL_CASES.length} cases`;
}
function filterDetailTable(){
    const q=document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase();
    const sf=document.getElementById('filterStatus').value;
    const cf=document.getElementById('filterCategory').value;
    const cof=document.getElementById('filterConfidence').value;
    let filtered=ALL_CASES.filter(c=>{
        if(sf&&c.status!==sf)return false;
        if(cf&&c.cat!==cf)return false;
        if(cof){if(cof==='HIGH'&&c.conf<90)return false;if(cof==='MEDIUM'&&(c.conf<50||c.conf>=90))return false;if(cof==='LOW'&&c.conf>=50)return false;}
        if(q){const h=(c.case+' '+c.cat+' '+c.expProv+' '+c.matchProv+' '+c.action+' '+c.reasoning+' '+c.status+' '+(c.changes||[]).join(' ')).toLowerCase();if(!h.includes(q))return false;}
        return true;
    });
    filtered.sort((a,b)=>{let va=a[detailSortField],vb=b[detailSortField];if(typeof va==='number')return detailSortAsc?va-vb:vb-va;va=String(va);vb=String(vb);return detailSortAsc?va.localeCompare(vb):vb.localeCompare(va);});
    buildDetailTable(filtered);
}
function sortDetailTable(field){if(detailSortField===field)detailSortAsc=!detailSortAsc;else{detailSortField=field;detailSortAsc=true;}filterDetailTable();}

function populateCatFilter(){const sel=document.getElementById('filterCategory');CATEGORIES.forEach(cat=>{const opt=document.createElement('option');opt.value=cat;opt.textContent=cat;sel.appendChild(opt);});}

// ============================================================
// CHARTS
// ============================================================
const catLabels=Object.keys(CAT_METRICS);
const catResRates=catLabels.map(c=>CAT_METRICS[c].resRate);
const catAvgConfs=catLabels.map(c=>CAT_METRICS[c].avgConf);
const catFailRates=catLabels.map(c=>CAT_METRICS[c].failRate);
const catBarColors=catLabels.map(c=>CAT_COLORS[c]||'#95a5a6');

const statusChart=new Chart(document.getElementById('statusChart'),{type:'doughnut',data:{labels:Object.keys(STATUS_COUNTS),datasets:[{data:Object.values(STATUS_COUNTS),backgroundColor:['#27ae60','#f39c12','#e74c3c','#c0392b','#8e44ad','#95a5a6'],borderWidth:3,borderColor:'#fff',hoverOffset:12}]},options:{responsive:true,plugins:{legend:{position:'bottom'},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:function(ctx){return ctx.label+': '+ctx.raw+' ('+(ctx.raw/300*100).toFixed(1)+'%)'}}}}} });
document.getElementById('statusChart').onclick=function(e){const pts=statusChart.getElementsAtEventForMode(e,'nearest',{intersect:true},true);if(pts.length)showKpiDrill(Object.keys(STATUS_COUNTS)[pts[0].index]);};

const actionChart=new Chart(document.getElementById('actionChart'),{type:'doughnut',data:{labels:Object.keys(ACTION_COUNTS),datasets:[{data:Object.values(ACTION_COUNTS),backgroundColor:['#27ae60','#f39c12','#e74c3c','#c0392b','#8e44ad','#95a5a6','#3498db'],borderWidth:3,borderColor:'#fff',hoverOffset:12}]},options:{responsive:true,plugins:{legend:{position:'bottom'},tooltip:{callbacks:{label:function(ctx){return ctx.label+': '+ctx.raw}}}}}});
document.getElementById('actionChart').onclick=function(e){const pts=actionChart.getElementsAtEventForMode(e,'nearest',{intersect:true},true);if(pts.length){const label=Object.keys(ACTION_COUNTS)[pts[0].index];openModal('Action: '+label,ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.action===label).length+' cases',ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.action===label));}};

new Chart(document.getElementById('confChart'),{type:'bar',data:{labels:Object.keys(CONF_BUCKETS),datasets:[{label:'Count',data:Object.values(CONF_BUCKETS),backgroundColor:['#e74c3c','#e67e22','#f1c40f','#3498db','#27ae60'],borderRadius:6}]},options:{responsive:true,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{y:{beginAtZero:true}},onClick:function(e,els){if(els.length){const bl=Object.keys(CONF_BUCKETS)[els[0].index];const ranges={"0-25%":[0,25],"26-50%":[26,50],"51-75%":[51,75],"76-90%":[76,90],"91-100%":[91,100]};const[lo,hi]=ranges[bl];openModal('Confidence: '+bl,ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.status!=='ERROR'&&c.status!=='PROMPT_LEAK'&&c.conf>=lo&&c.conf<=hi).length+' cases',ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.status!=='ERROR'&&c.status!=='PROMPT_LEAK'&&c.conf>=lo&&c.conf<=hi));}}}});

new Chart(document.getElementById('catResChart'),{type:'bar',data:{labels:catLabels,datasets:[{label:'Resolution %',data:catResRates,backgroundColor:catBarColors,borderRadius:6}]},options:{responsive:true,indexAxis:'y',plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{x:{beginAtZero:true,max:100}},onClick:function(e,els){if(els.length)showCategoryDrill(catLabels[els[0].index]);}}});

new Chart(document.getElementById('catConfChart'),{type:'bar',data:{labels:catLabels,datasets:[{label:'Avg Confidence %',data:catAvgConfs,backgroundColor:catBarColors,borderRadius:6}]},options:{responsive:true,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{y:{beginAtZero:true,max:100}},onClick:function(e,els){if(els.length)showCategoryDrill(catLabels[els[0].index]);}}});

new Chart(document.getElementById('catFailChart'),{type:'bar',data:{labels:catLabels,datasets:[{label:'Failure %',data:catFailRates,backgroundColor:'#e74c3c',borderRadius:6}]},options:{responsive:true,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{y:{beginAtZero:true}},onClick:function(e,els){if(els.length){const cat=catLabels[els[0].index];const cases=ALL_CASES.filter(c=>c.cat===cat&&(c.status==='ERROR'||c.status==='PROMPT_LEAK'));openModal('Failures: '+cat,cases.length+' cases',cases);}}}});

document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){if(e.key==='Escape'){closeCaseModal();closeModal();}});

buildCatTable();populateCatFilter();filterDetailTable();
</script>
</body>
</html>
